Abstract: Decoherence is the biggest bottleneck in all quantum technologies. For nitrogen-vacancy (NV) centers in diamond, the loss of coherence is caused by the electron and nuclear spin bath of the diamond lattice. Here, we demonstrate that the spin bath - that typically causes decoherence - entangles the spin states of the NV electron and the host $^{14}$N nucleus. The many-body interaction between the $^{14}$N nucleus - electron - bath spins at an energy level anti-crossing occurring for an applied magnetic field orientation perpendicular to the NV axis is responsible for this effect. This is observed experimentally on NV ensembles via electron spin-echo measurements, where the echo envelope is modulated at the frequency of a $^{14}$N nuclear spin transition. Using numerical simulations, we show that the spin bath coupling to the NV centers is essential for observing this modulation. Due to the zero first-order Zeeman effect at the anti-crossing, the observed oscillations have long spin-echo coherence times, 2--3 times those at the parallel magnetic field orientation. The oscillation frequency is highly stable and robust against environmental fluctuations. These findings provide new opportunities for fundamental studies of many-body physics and quantum sensing.

</details>


### [96] [Quantum percolation theory for dynamic propagation connectivity of transport networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30218)
*Junxiang Xu,Chence Niu,Divya Jayakumar Nair,Vinayak Dixit*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出动态传播连通性（DPC），将量子渗透理论引入交通网络韧性分析，刻画网络在扰动下传播能力的功能性退化，并建立了包括谱分析框架、分离定理等在内的完整理论体系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于渗流理论和拓扑连通性的方法仅关注路径是否存在与连通组件是否碎裂，无法捕捉网络拓扑完整但传播能力已显著下降的功能性退化。

Method: 将量子渗透理论引入交通网络连通性分析，将扰动下的交通网络映射为传播算子系统，建立DPC的谱分析框架，定义时间平均参与指数作为核心量化指标。

Result: DPC在均匀扰动下保持不变，在异质扰动下退化；建立了退化率与传播算子最小特征值间距及异质扰动强度之间的定量关系；证明了DPC与代数连通性的分离定理；给出了环图上DPC的解析表达式及二阶摄动近似；三个交通基准网络的数值实验验证了所有理论结论，并确认了退化单调性、与代数连通性的分离以及网络规模对退化的放大效应。

Conclusion: 本文提供了一个超越拓扑连通性的交通网络韧性评估理论框架。

Abstract: Connectivity degradation in transport networks under structural disturbance is a central problem in network resilience research. Existing methods rely mainly on percolation theory and topological connectivity measures. They focus on whether paths exist and whether connected components fragment. These approaches cannot capture functional degradation where network topology remains intact but propagation ability has already declined substantially. This paper introduces quantum percolation theory into transport network connectivity analysis and proposes Dynamic Propagation Connectivity (DPC) as a new measure that characterises network propagation ability under disturbance. By mapping a transport network under disturbance into a propagation operator system, this paper establishes a spectral analysis framework for DPC and defines the time-averaged participation index as its core quantification. This paper provides a series of rigorous theoretical results. DPC remains constant under homogeneous disturbance and degrades under heterogeneous disturbance. This paper establishes a quantitative relationship between the degradation rate, the minimum eigenvalue spacing of the propagation operator, and heterogeneous deviation strength. This paper proves a separation theorem between DPC and algebraic connectivity. It derives an analytical expression for DPC and a second-order perturbation approximation on the ring graph. Numerical experiments on three transport benchmark networks verify all theoretical conclusions and confirm degradation monotonicity, separation from algebraic connectivity, and degradation amplification by network size. This paper provides a theoretical framework for transport network resilience assessment that goes beyond topological connectivity.

</details>


### [97] [Quantum percolation based dynamic propagation connectivity for critical-area identification in transport networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30242)
*Junxiang Xu,Chence Niu,Vinayak Dixit,Divya Jayakumar Nair,Tingting Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出基于量子渗流的动态传播连通性（DPC）指标，将链路旅行时间转化为连续传播强度，通过区域退化实验识别交通网络中导致传播能力损失最大的关键空间区域，并在Sioux Falls基准网络和六组佛罗里达飓风Irma后的路网中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统渗流方法基于二元连通性识别设施故障或拓扑断裂，但无法捕捉链路运行条件逐渐退化导致的网络传播能力损失，且对哪些空间区域造成最大的网络范围传播能力下降缺乏诊断工具。

Method: 构建时变链路旅行时间到连续传播强度的映射，定义埃尔米特传播算子；通过区域退化实验测量候选区域移除后DPC的损失值，从而量化各区域对网络传播能力的贡献。

Result: 基准测试验证DPC能识别预定义的结构关键走廊；在佛罗里达路网中，DPC识别的关键区域与基于链路计数、局部退化、边介数、代数连通性和经典渗流的方法显著不同；网络1-4中DPC与经典渗流排序呈负斯皮尔曼相关；鲁棒性检验中平均排序一致性达0.84-0.96。

Conclusion: 将渗流分析从二元连通性损失拓展到连续传播退化，为区域监测、应急规划和恢复优先级排序提供了基于空间诊断的工具。

Abstract: Transport networks often lose functionality through gradual degradation in link operating conditions before topological disconnection occurs. Link-centred and binary percolation measures identify important facilities or connectivity failures, but they provide limited information on which spatial areas cause the largest loss of network-wide propagation capability. This paper develops a Dynamic Propagation Connectivity (DPC) metric based on quantum percolation for critical-area identification in transport networks. Time-varying link travel times are converted into continuous propagation strengths, which define a Hermitian propagation operator at each observation time. Candidate regions are then evaluated by a regional degradation experiment that measures the resulting loss of DPC. The method is applied to a benchmark Sioux Falls network and six Florida road networks during the post-Hurricane Irma disruption and recovery period, using 1,281 five-minute observation times. The benchmark confirms that the regional DPC score identifies a predefined structurally critical corridor. In the Florida networks, the identified critical areas differ from regions selected by link count, local degradation, edge betweenness, algebraic connectivity, and classical percolation. In Networks 1 to 4, DPC and classical percolation rankings have negative Spearman correlations, showing that continuous propagation degradation and binary fragmentation reveal different vulnerability patterns. Robustness tests under alternative travel time scaling, degradation strength, and grid size show stable results, with mean rank agreement between 0.84 and 0.96. The findings extend transport resilience analysis based on percolation from binary connectivity loss to continuous propagation degradation and provide a spatial diagnostic tool for regional monitoring, emergency planning, and recovery prioritisation.

</details>


### [98] [Action on the Sphere: An Interfering Mean-Field Propagator for the Bose-Hubbard Dimer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30276)
*Elana F Todd-Miller,Eva-Maria Graefe*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文基于平均场近似，通过构建初值相干态传播子并考虑相位贡献，成功再现了Bose-Hubbard二聚体中的崩溃与恢复动力学及多粒子隧穿效应，并可将方法推广至更一般的SU(2)和SU(M)系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管Bose-Hubbard系统的平均场近似已被广泛使用，但标准平均场方法无法捕捉多粒子量子动力学中的恢复、崩溃和隧穿等干涉效应，因此需要一种能够保留相位信息的半经典方法。

Method: 从时间依赖变分原理出发，假设多粒子波函数为SU(2)相干态，构建初值相干态传播子；通过对平均场轨迹求和并保留其对应的平均场作用量相位，再叠加上时间切片过程来精确描述多粒子隧穿效应。

Result: 该方法能够再现Bose-Hubbard二聚体中的崩溃与恢复动力学，并且通过时间切片过程准确捕获了多粒子隧穿效应。

Conclusion: 本文发展的半经典传播子方法有效桥接了平均场近似与完整量子动力学之间的差距，适用于更一般的SU(2)哈密顿量，并可推广至SU(M)系统。

Abstract: The Bose-Hubbard system has been studied extensively both theoretically and experimentally, in particular in the context of ultracold atomic gases in optical lattices. Even in the two-mode case the many-particle dynamics display complex interference effects resulting in revival and breakdown phenomena as well as tunnelling. The most basic theoretical description is the mean-field approximation, which can be derived from a time-dependent variational principle assuming the many-particle wave function is an SU(2) coherent state. Here we build on this to construct a simple initial-value coherent state propagator, summing over mean-field trajectories and keeping track of their phases, given by the corresponding mean-field actions. This yields an approximation to the full time-dependent many-particle state, and is able to reproduce breakdown and revival dynamics. Applying a time-slicing procedure on top of this, we are able to accurately capture many-particle tunnelling effects. While in this paper we focus our analysis on the Bose-Hubbard dimer, the methods developed can be applied to more general SU(2) Hamiltonians, and can be extended to SU(M) systems.

</details>


### [99] [Quantum Lazy Sampling and Path Recording for Any Group](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30281)
*Ben Foxman,Alex Lombardi,Fermi Ma,Barak Nehoran,John Wright*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于第一性原理的通用的路径记录预言机（path-recording oracle），可在量子算法中完美模拟任意闭子群 $U(N)$ 的随机群元素，并基于群张量幂表示的交换子代数描述更新规则。该预言机具有可解释性，记录了算法通过查询学到的信息，并可用于不同群压缩预言机之间的直接比较。作为应用，通过比较 $S_N$ 和 $U(N)$ 得到了目前最简洁的伪随机酉构造：伪随机排列与随机 Clifford 的乘积 PC，改进了先前的 PFC 构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在量子算法和密码学中，核心挑战之一是分析具有对随机群元素（如随机函数、置换或酉算子）的预言机访问权限的算法。经典工具“惰性采样”允许预言机在查询过程中逐步采样部分信息，但缺乏对应的量子版本。尽管已有针对特定群（如随机函数、酉算子、置换）的压缩预言机（Zhandry CRYPTO '19, Ma-Huang STOC '25, Carolan STOC '26），但仍缺少一个通用的、可解释的、能从第一性原理推导的压缩预言机。此外，Grinko-Yoshida (QIP '26) 给出的通用压缩预言机缺乏清晰的可解释性。因此，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 从第一性原理出发，定义并分析一种通用的路径记录预言机。该预言机以叠加态存储 t 个输入-输出对，其更新规则由群张量幂表示的交换子代数描述，从而透明地记录算法已学习的信息。该预言机适用于 $U(N)$ 的任意闭子群，并能完美模拟随机群元素。通过比较不同群（如 $S_N$ 和 $U(N)$）的压缩预言机，导出伪随机性的新证明技术。

Result: 1. 构建了通用的、可解释的路径记录预言机，能完美模拟任意闭子群 $U(N)$ 的随机元素。
2. 通过比较 $S_N$ 和 $U(N)$ 的压缩预言机，得到了目前最简洁的伪随机酉（PRU）构造：伪随机排列与随机 Clifford 的乘积 PC，改进了之前更复杂的 PFC 构造。

Conclusion: 本文提出的路径记录预言机首次从第一性原理出发，统一了多种已有特定群的压缩预言机，并提供了可解释性。它为量子密码学中的安全性证明和下界推导提供了有力新工具。特别地，其在伪随机酉构造上的应用展示了简洁性和有效性，为量子密码学中的伪随机性研究提供了新思路。

Abstract: A central challenge in quantum algorithms and cryptography is reasoning about algorithms with oracle access to a random group element (e.g. a random function, permutation, or unitary). Can we efficiently simulate such algorithms? Can we determine what they know after t queries? A classical tool for this is lazy sampling: the oracle does not commit to the full group element upfront, but rather samples partial information about it on the fly. We study a quantum analog of lazy sampling: compressed oracles (or recording oracles). These are quantum data structures that allow on-the-fly simulation for quantum queries, originally introduced by Zhandry (CRYPTO '19) for random functions, and generalized to unitaries by Ma-Huang (STOC '25) and permutations by Carolan (STOC '26), and used to great effect in security proofs and lower bounds due to their interpretability.
  We define and analyze a general-purpose and interpretable path-recording oracle, derived from first principles, that perfectly simulates random elements of any closed subgroup of $U(N)$. Our oracle stores, in superposition, t input-output pairs, with updates described in terms of the commutant of the group's tensor power representation. This transparently records the information the algorithm has learned. Our oracle builds on recent work of Grinko-Yoshida (QIP '26), who gave a different general-purpose compressed oracle without clear interpretability.
  One interesting application of our path-recording is allowing direct comparisons between compressed oracles of different groups, giving a new technique for proving pseudorandomness results. For example, comparing $S_N$ and $U(N)$ yields what is arguably the simplest construction to date of pseudorandom unitaries: the product PC of a pseudorandom permutation and a random Clifford, improving on the prior PFC construction (Metger-Poremba-Sinha-Yuen, FOCS '24; Ma-Huang, STOC '25).

</details>


### [100] [Thermometry with multilevel transmon probes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30302)
*Antonio Mandarino,Matteo G. A. Paris,Claudia Benedetti*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了超越二能级近似的transmon系统在纳米测温中的量子极限，比较了量子比特、谐振子和Duffing振子三种模型的热计量性能，发现多级非简谐结构增强了温度估计精度，并指出了标准四阶截断的局限性，提出了有界非简谐模型以提高测温鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transmon系统因其对环境涨落的高灵敏度而成为纳米测温的理想平台，但其内在的非简谐性（用于量子比特操作）导致能谱非等距，显著影响热布居和测温灵敏度。现有研究多局限于二能级近似，未能充分揭示多能级结构对热计量精度的影响。

Method: 比较三种互补模型（量子比特、谐振子、弱非简谐Duffing振子），计算量子Fisher信息（QFI）随温度的变化；引入有界非简谐模型（受限四次势和六次修正项）以解决标准四次截断势能无下界的问题。

Result: 包含更高激发态可增强系统温度信息提取的最大量；标准四阶截断因势能无下界仅支持虚假亚稳态；有界非简谐模型提升了Duffing regime之外的测温鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 为transmon基纳米测温提供了实用指导，阐明了非简谐多能级谱在量子温度估计中的作用。

Abstract: Superconducting transmon systems are promising platforms for nanoscale thermometry due to their high sensitivity to environmental fluctuations. Their intrinsic anharmonicity, which is essential for qubit operations, gives rise to a non-equidistant energy spectrum that significantly affects the thermal populations and, consequently, the thermometric sensitivity. In this work, we investigate the ultimate quantum limits of temperature estimation with a transmon beyond the two-level approximation. We compare the thermometric performance of three complementary models: the qubit, a harmonic oscillator and a weakly anharmonic Duffing oscillator, evaluating their corresponding quantum Fisher information (QFI) as a function of the temperature. We show that the multilevel anharmonic structure of the transmon affects its thermometric precision. Indeed, including higher excited states enhances the maximum amount of information that can be extracted about the system temperature, compared to a qubit probe. Furthermore, we address a fundamental limitation of the standard quartic truncation, which yields a potential that is unbounded from below and supports only spurious metastable states. By introducing bounded anharmonic models, namely a confined quartic potential and a sextic correction term, we assess the robustness of the thermometric precision beyond the Duffing regime. Our results provide practical guidelines for transmon-based nanoscale thermometry and clarify the role of the anharmonic multilevel spectrum in quantum temperature estimation.

</details>


### [101] [Phase-Altered Interleaved Randomized Benchmarking for Compiled Quantum Gates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30327)
*Simona K. Grigorova,Nikolay V. Vitanov,Boyan T. Torosov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出相位改变交错随机基准测试（PA-IRB）协议，用于检测虚拟相位门是否显著影响IRB误差估计。在IBM超导处理器上的Toffoli门案例研究中，PA-IRB显示Δr在不确定度范围内与零一致，表明虚拟相位添加或移除不会可测量地改变IRB导出的误差估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准IRB要求交错门为Clifford门以确保可扩展误差估计，但超导处理器中的许多非Clifford相位门通过软件定义的帧更新实现（虚拟相位），而非实际控制脉冲。这引发了问题：插入或移除此类虚拟相位是否会可测量地改变IRB误差估计？现有方法缺乏对此类抽象层感知的诊断能力。

Method: 提出PA-IRB协议，这是一种配对IRB诊断协议，比较源自同一编译实现的相位剥离（phase-stripped）和相位修饰（phase-dressed）Clifford交错门。通过计算Δr = r_d - r_s及其组合不确定度，检验虚拟相位门是否在统计误差之外影响IRB衰减。在IBM超导处理器上对编译后的Toffoli门进行案例研究，其中T/T†门通过虚拟Z旋转实现。

Result: 在多个校准运行中，Δr在不确定度范围内与零一致，表明在所使用的编译和执行栈下，虚拟相位添加或移除不会可测量地改变IRB导出的误差估计。

Conclusion: PA-IRB提供了一种轻量级、抽象感知的诊断工具，用于涉及软件定义相位操作的基准测试工作流。该配对比较方法还可用于对编译门误差中非Clifford成分的贡献设置操作界限，即使这些成分是物理执行而非虚拟实现的。

Abstract: Interleaved randomized benchmarking (IRB) provides a scalable estimate of a gate's error rate, but its standard guarantees require the interleaved gate to be Clifford~\cite{Magesan2012Interleaved,magesan2012characterizing}. In superconducting processors, many non-Clifford phase gates in compiled circuits are implemented virtually as software-defined frame updates rather than as additional control pulses~\cite{mckay2017efficient}. This raises the question of whether inserting or removing such virtual phases measurably changes IRB error estimates.
  We introduce \emph{phase-altered interleaved randomized benchmarking} (PA-IRB), a paired-IRB diagnostic protocol comparing phase-stripped and phase-dressed Clifford interleaving gates derived from the same compiled implementation. PA-IRB reports $Δr=r_d-r_s$ with combined uncertainty to test whether virtual phase gates affect the extracted IRB decay beyond statistical error.
  As a case study, we apply PA-IRB to a compiled Toffoli gate executed on IBM superconducting processors, where the constituent $T/T^\dagger$ gates are implemented as virtual $Z$ rotations. Across tested calibration runs, $Δr$ is consistent with zero within uncertainty, indicating that virtual phase addition or removal does not measurably alter the IRB-derived error estimate under the employed compilation and execution stack. More generally, PA-IRB provides a lightweight, abstraction-aware diagnostic for benchmarking workflows involving software-defined phase operations. The same paired comparison can also be used to place operational bounds on the contribution of non-Clifford components to the compiled gate error, even when those components are physically executed rather than implemented virtually.

</details>


### [102] [Learning the structure of open quantum systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30358)
*Laura Lewis,Ewin Tang,John Wright*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种学习n量子比特恒定局域Lindbladian系数的算法，总演化时间O(g d² log(n)/ε²)，实现了与哈密顿量学习算法同等的理想特性：非自适应、无辅助比特的随机Pauli测量电路，无需预知结构，支持准局域和幂律Lindbladian学习。算法是简单迭代方法，目标函数由限制在少位点区域的Lindbladian傅里叶系数构成，并识别了开放系统的独特困难——"混淆"项。在混淆有限时性能提升，展示了从实时演化进行哈密顿量结构学习的新算法（更简单），以及首个从高温Gibbs态进行哈密顿量结构学习的高效算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 设计一种高效、通用的开放量子系统（Lindbladian）学习算法，克服开放系统特有的混淆项难题，同时满足哈密顿量学习算法已实现的非自适应、无辅助比特、无需先验知识等理想特性，并拓展到准局域和幂律系统。

Method: 采用简单迭代方法，目标函数为限制在少位点区域的Lindbladian傅里叶系数。使用非自适应、无辅助比特的随机Pauli测量电路，时间分辨率为Θ(1/g)。通过分析识别开放系统特有的"混淆"项，在混淆有限时性能提升。应用该框架于哈密顿量结构学习（从实时演化和高温Gibbs态）。

Result: 总演化时间复杂度为O(g d² log(n)/ε²)，其中g为单站点能量，d为相互作用图的近似度。算法无需预知Lindbladian结构，支持准局域和幂律Lindbladian。从实时演化进行哈密顿量结构学习的新算法显著简化；首个从高温Gibbs态进行哈密顿量结构学习的高效算法。

Conclusion: 所提Lindbladian学习算法实现了与哈密顿量学习相媲美的理想特性集，解决了开放系统特有的混淆难题，并拓展了哈密顿量结构学习的新方法（从实时演化和高温Gibbs态），具有重要的理论意义和实际应用价值。

Abstract: We design an algorithm for learning the coefficients of an $n$-qubit constant-local Lindbladian to $\varepsilon$ error with $O(g d^2 \log(n) / \varepsilon^2)$ total evolution time, where $g$ is the single-site energy and $d$ is the (approximate) degree of the interaction graph. Though Lindbladians present new challenges not present in the special case of Hamiltonians, our algorithm achieves the suite of desiderata attained by state-of-the-art Hamiltonian learning algorithms: (1) it uses non-adaptive, ancilla-free randomized Pauli measurement circuits with a time resolution of only $Θ(1/g)$; (2) it works without knowledge of the structure of the unknown Lindbladian; (3) it depends on a smooth form of degree, thereby supporting the learning of quasi-local and power-law Lindbladians.
  Our algorithm is a simple iterative method, where the objective function consists of Fourier coefficients of the Lindbladian restricted to few-site regions. Its analysis identifies the difficulty unique to open systems, which we call "confusing" terms. For settings where the "confusion" is limited, the performance of the algorithm improves. We demonstrate this for the case of structure learning of Hamiltonians from access to real-time evolution, where we obtain a new algorithm that is significantly simpler than previous work. In addition, using the same iterative method, we design the first efficient algorithm for structure learning Hamiltonians from high-temperature Gibbs states.

</details>


### [103] [Provable Quantum Advantage for Dynamical Phase Transition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30396)
*Jue Xu,Xiao Yuan,Qi Zhao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了动态量子相变（DQPT）的检测复杂性。证明了一般DQPT的估计即使对量子计算也难解，而子系统变体的判定等价于通用量子电路模拟（具备指数级量子优势）。针对局域DQPT的临界时间搜索，提出了二次加速的量子算法，可实现海森堡极限精度且时间点亚线性扩展。此外，将经典演化编码为量子动力学，可为经典系统的异常现象检测带来量子加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管DQPT作为非平衡量子相变的理论已被广泛研究，但其可检测性的计算复杂性（包括经典和量子计算资源）尚未被探索。本文旨在填补这一空白，并探索量子算法在DQPT检测中的优势。

Method: 1. 通过复杂性理论证明一般DQPT估计的难解性；2. 展示子系统DQPT判定与通用量子电路模拟的等价性；3. 设计基于量子算法的局域DQPT临界时间搜索方案，实现海森堡极限精度（精度与时间T成反比）和时间点数量的亚线性扩展；4. 通过将经典演化编码为量子动力学，将框架扩展至经典系统。

Result: 1. 一般DQPT估计是量子难解的；2. 子系统DQPT判定具有指数级量子优势；3. 局域DQPT临界时间搜索算法二次加速，达到海森堡极限精度；4. 量子优势可迁移用于检测经典系统的异常现象。

Conclusion: DQPT的检测在经典和量子计算之间表现出显著的复杂性层级：一般情况难解，子系统变体具备指数量子优势，而局域情况可通过专用量子算法高效求解。所提框架还为经典动力学的异常检测开辟了量子加速的新途径。

Abstract: The universal scaling of critical behavior in phase transitions is a cornerstone of physics. Dynamical quantum phase transitions (DQPTs) are their nonequilibrium analogues: abrupt nonanalyticities that emerge as a quantum system evolves in time. Yet the hardness and cost of detecting this phenomenon remain largely unexplored. We prove that estimating DQPT to a certain precision is intractable even for quantum computers, whereas deciding a subsystem variant of DQPT is as hard as simulating generic quantum circuits, implying a provable exponential quantum advantage. Furthermore, to search for critical times of local DQPTs, we show a quadratically faster quantum algorithm that estimates observables of Hamiltonian dynamics at multiple time points with Heisenberg-limited precision and sublinear scaling in the number of time points. Moreover, through encoding classical evolution into quantum dynamics, our framework enables broader quantum speedups for detecting anomalous phenomena in classical systems.

</details>


### [104] [Quantum-enhanced Monte Carlo Tree Search framework for combinatorial optimization problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30415)
*Yohan Finet,Yves Bérubé-Lauzière,Victor Drouin-Touchette*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出了AtomTreeSearch，一种将中性原子量子计算机上可原生实现的量子子程序嵌入蒙特卡洛树搜索框架的混合经典-量子算法，在旅行商问题上（最多60个随机城市和100个TSPLIB城市）匹配或优于OR-Tools和模拟退火，表明将量子子程序嵌入经典搜索框架是实现近期量子效用的有前途路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子优化方法（Grover算法电路深度过大不适用于NISQ设备，量子退火无法处理实际问题规模）难以在近期量子设备上有效解决组合优化问题，亟需一种能在当前量子硬件上发挥效用的混合方法。

Method: 将中性原子量子计算机上可原生实现的最大加权独立集量子子程序嵌入蒙特卡洛树搜索框架：在每个扩展步骤中，量子处理器提供候选动作的最大加权独立集，选择后执行这些集体动作以获得子节点。

Result: 在随机欧几里得实例（最多60城市）和TSPLIB实例（最多100城市）上，混合算法通常匹配或优于OR-Tools和模拟退火；量子子程序相比经典替代子程序能产生更多样化且更高质量的分支。

Conclusion: 精心设计的量子子程序嵌入经典搜索框架是实现近期组合优化量子效用的有前途路径。

Abstract: Over the past decades, the operations research community has developed numerous effective optimization algorithms, yet quantum computing is emerging as a new computational paradigm with the potential to approach optimization problems more efficiently. Grover's algorithm offers a provable speedup for combinatorial optimization, but its circuit depth places it beyond current noisy intermediate-scale quantum (NISQ) devices. A more accessible alternative is to reformulate the optimization problem as a quadratic unconstrained binary optimization (QUBO) problem and apply quantum annealing; however, practical problem instances remain out of reach for existing hardware. We introduce AtomTreeSearch, a hybrid classical-quantum algorithm that integrates a quantum subroutine natively implementable on neutral-atom quantum computers within a Monte Carlo Tree Search framework. At each expansion step, a maximal weighted independent set of candidate actions provided by the quantum processor is selected, and these collective actions are performed to obtain a child node. We benchmark our method on the Traveling Salesman Problem, with instances of up to 60 cities on random Euclidean instances and up to 100 cities on TSPLIB instances. Our hybrid algorithm generally matches or outperforms both OR-Tools and simulated annealing on these instances, and we find that the quantum subroutine produces more diverse and higher-quality branches compared to classical alternate subroutines. These results suggest that carefully scoped quantum subroutines embedded in classical search frameworks represent a promising path toward near-term quantum utility in combinatorial optimization.

</details>


### [105] [Stable Qubit Readout and the Identifiability of Population Change](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30462)
*Dongdong Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文分析了量子比特读取中，稳定的读取统计量虽常被视为物理响应的证据，但稳定性本身并不能唯一确定状态量的变化。针对有限量子比特集合的二进制读取，重点研究了计算基 population 变化能否从可重复的响应数据中可靠识别。结果表明，答案由校准后的测量方向决定，而非循环一致性。对于完全校准的有限读取族，推导了所有兼容 population 变化的精确闭区间；并通过联合可测量的实例展示，相同的概率和循环检查可同时支持正、零、负三种 population 解释。当仅信任对角读取增益和相干敏感性上界时，获得了尖锐的极小极大区间及充要符号条件 g>2χ。这些结果将实现稳定性与 population 可识别性分离，为量子比特读取校准提供了分析基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文指出，稳定的读取统计量常被作为存在明确定义的物理响应的证据，但这种稳定性本身并不能唯一确定是哪个状态量发生了变化。这一根本性ambiguity在量子测量领域尚未被充分分析，尤其是对于由二进制读取测量的有限量子比特集合，核心动机在于厘清何时可重复的响应数据能够可靠地证明底层 population 变化的符号或范围。

Method: 论文从理论上分析了有限量子比特集合的二进制读取问题，聚焦于计算基 population 的变化。通过区分"校准的测量方向"与"循环一致性"两个概念，推导了完全校准有限读取族下的精确闭区间公式，并构造了联合可测量的反例。同时，在对角读取增益和相干敏感性有界假设下，运用极小极大分析得到了最优区间和充要符号条件。

Result: 获得了两个关键结果：(1) 对于完全校准的有限读取族，推导了所有兼容 population 变化的精确闭区间；(2) 当仅信任对角读取增益 g 和相干敏感性上界 χ 时，得到了尖锐的极小极大区间以及 population 变化符号可识别的充要条件 g > 2χ。此外，通过实例展示了相同概率和循环检查可同时支持正、零、负三种 population 解释。

Conclusion: 论文明确分离了"实现稳定性"与"population 可识别性"两个概念，指出稳定性本身不足以确定 population 变化，关键在于测量方向的校准而非循环一致性。这些结果为量子比特读取校准提供了严格的分析基准，并提示实验者在解读稳定读取统计量时必须考虑校准信息，否则可能得出错误的 population 变化结论。

Abstract: Stable readout statistics are often taken as evidence for a well-defined physical response, but stability alone need not identify which state quantity has changed. We analyze this issue for finite collections of qubit states measured by binary readouts, focusing on changes in computational-basis population. The central question is when reproducible response data certify the sign or range of an underlying population change. We show that the answer is controlled by the calibrated measurement directions, not by loop consistency alone. For a fully calibrated finite readout family, we derive an exact closed-form interval of all compatible population changes. We also construct a same-record, jointly measurable example in which identical probabilities and accepted loop checks admit positive, zero, and negative population interpretations. When only a diagonal readout gain and a bound on coherence sensitivity are trusted, we obtain the sharp minimax interval and the necessary-and-sufficient sign condition $g>2χ$. These results separate implementation stability from population identifiability and provide analytic benchmarks for qubit readout calibration.

</details>


### [106] [Demonstration of unpartible entanglement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30468)
*Philip Held,Laura Ares,Federico Pegoraro,Jonas Lammers,Benjamin Brecht,Jan Sperling,Christine Silberhorn*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文首次实验验证了模式无关纠缠（mode-independent entanglement），即纠缠在模式基变换下依然保持，并通过可重构时间复用干涉仪和测量诱导非线性产生纠缠双光子态，利用定制量子态层析成像验证了高保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统纠缠依赖于预定义的参与者（模式）划分，当模式定义改变时纠缠可能消失，这限制了纠缠在现实量子通信（如噪声环境、不可信方）中的应用。为解决此问题，需要实现一种对模式变换鲁棒的纠缠类型。

Method: 实验方案基于全可重构的时间复用干涉仪，结合测量诱导非线性产生纠缠双光子态；认证过程采用定制的量子态层析成像技术，验证纠缠在所有正交模式基下均保持不变。

Result: 成功生成了纠缠双光子态，并在所有模式基选择下验证了模式无关纠缠的存在，获得了高保真度结果。

Conclusion: 模式无关纠缠是一种更具鲁棒性和操作优势的量子关联，能在模式变换下保持纠缠特性，对未来量子通信等实际应用具有重要意义。

Abstract: We report on the first experimental verification of mode-independent entanglement. Commonly, the entanglement of a state is firmly based on pre-defined parties that are correlated, and the state might be disentangled when the definition of the parties is changed. Exceeding this party-dependent concept, we realize a type of quantum entanglement that persists even if the parties, in our case modes, are transformed. This safeguards the performance of entanglement in real-world applications, such as quantum communication settings involving noise and untrusted parties. For the state generation, we present an experimental scheme based on a fully reconfigurable temporally multiplexed interferometer with measurement-induced nonlinearities, which generates heralded two-photon states in two modes that are entangled for all choices of orthonormal mode basis. For the certification process, we utilize a tailored quantum-state tomography, achieving fidelities that validate the presence of mode-independent entanglement as a resilient and operationally advantageous quantum correlation.

</details>


### [107] [Staged Hybridisation for Visual Quantum Reinforcement Learning via Knowledge Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30520)
*Javier Lazaro,Juan-Ignacio Vazquez,Pablo Garcia-Bringas*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出将知识蒸馏（KD）作为一种分阶段混合策略用于视觉量子强化学习（QRL）：先训练经典视觉教师模型并冻结其编码器，再将教师策略行为蒸馏到紧凑的下游头（经典或基于变分量子电路），使浅层量子头能在像素级任务中习得非平凡行为，避免了端到端训练的困难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉环境中高维观测、不稳定的RL优化与受约束的VQC难以联合训练，端到端混合训练极其困难，因此需要一种分阶段混合策略来解耦视觉表示学习与量子策略学习。

Method: 分阶段知识蒸馏：1) 先训练经典视觉教师模型；2) 冻结教师编码器作为特征接口；3) 将教师策略行为蒸馏到紧凑的下游头（经典或VQC头，包括角度编码和幅度编码两种量子头）。

Result: 在CartPole Pixels和Acrobot Pixels上，分阶段KD使浅层VQC头能在像素任务中习得非平凡视觉控制行为；角度编码VQC头接近教师性能；幅度编码VQC头极端紧凑但更脆弱、对预算敏感、仿真时间更长。

Conclusion: 分阶段KD将视觉QRL重新定义为紧凑头学习问题，为在标准端到端RL循环之外训练小型量子兼容策略开辟了实用路径。

Abstract: Visual environments are a demanding setting for quantum reinforcement learning (QRL): high-dimensional observations, unstable RL optimisation, and constrained variational quantum circuits (VQCs) are difficult to train jointly. This paper studies knowledge distillation (KD) as a staged hybridisation strategy for visual QRL. Instead of training a hybrid visual agent end-to-end from pixels, we first train a classical visual teacher, freeze its encoder as a feature interface, and distil the teacher's policy behaviour into compact downstream heads. These heads can be classical or VQC-based, enabling small quantum-compatible students to be evaluated under the same frozen representation as compact classical controls.
  We evaluate the pipeline on CartPole Pixels and Acrobot Pixels. The results show that staged KD enables shallow VQC heads to acquire non-trivial visual-control behaviour in settings where direct pixel-based training would be substantially more difficult. Angle-encoded VQC heads retain near-teacher performance, while amplitude-encoded heads push compactness to an extreme regime, at the cost of greater fragility, stronger budget sensitivity, and higher simulation time. Overall, staged KD reframes visual QRL as a compact-head learning problem, opening a practical route for training small quantum-compatible policies outside the standard end-to-end RL loop.

</details>


### [108] [Working with measurement-based computations on qudits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30525)
*Piotr Mitosek,Miriam Backens*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文对基于测量的量子计算中的qudit流结构给出了更简洁的定义，证明了聚焦流的典范性，将流查找算法复杂度从O(n⁴)改进到O(n³)，探索了多种保流变换，并提出了生成大规模qudit计算流的算法方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原有的qudit流定义比qubit流定义更复杂、更难以处理，需要简化定义并推广qubit流中已有的有用性质。

Method: 重新定义qudit流，证明聚焦流的典范性；改进代数形式化描述以捕获聚焦流；提出O(n³)的流查找算法；探索多种保流变换（枢轴变换、顶点删除/插入、流可逆性）；提出大规模qudit计算流的生成算法。

Result: 给出了更简洁的qudit流定义；证明了聚焦流是典范的；算法复杂度从O(n⁴)降到O(n³)；建立了多种保流变换；提出了生成大规模qudit流的算法方法。

Conclusion: 简化了qudit流理论，为qudit测量式量子计算的优化和实际应用奠定了基础。

Abstract: Measurement-based quantum computing is a universal model of quantum computation in which successive product measurements of an entangled resource state drive the computation. The non-deterministic nature of measurements necessitates adaptivity to ensure an overall deterministic computation. Flow structures characterise cases in which such an adaptive correction procedure is possible. Recently, flow has been defined in a setting where the resource states are prime-dimensional qudit graph states rather than the usual qubit graph states. Yet, this qudit flow definition is more burdensome to work with than analogous definitions for qubits.
  Here, we give a simpler definition of qudit flow and consider various useful properties of this flow, drawing on results for the qubit case. In particular, we show how to focus qudit flow and argue that focused flow is canonical. We improve the previous algebraic formulation to capture focused flow and use it to obtain an $O(n^3)$ flow-finding algorithm (where $n$ is the number of qudits), matching the best known complexity for qubit flows and improving on the previous $O(n^4)$ result for qudits. Furthermore, we explore multiple flow-preserving transformations, thus opening a pathway to using flow for optimisation. These transformations include pivoting, removal and insertion of certain types of vertices, and reversibility of flow. Lastly, we propose an algorithmic approach to generating large qudit computations with flow, for testing or machine learning.

</details>


### [109] [Untangling QLDPC Codes with Biased Noise Ancilla](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30592)
*Runjiang Bi,Kathleen Chang,Shruti Puri*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种硬件感知方法，通过使用偏置噪声辅助量子比特（ancilla）来避免QLDPC码中的钩错误（hook errors）和短循环，显著提升逻辑错误率性能，即使在无法维持完全偏置的条件下也能实现近一个数量级的改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QLDPC码虽然在高速率、高距离方面取得显著进展，但存在两个关键问题：(1) 难以设计低深度的症候提取电路，避免错误从辅助量子比特传播到多个数据量子比特（即钩错误）；(2) 基于置信传播的译码器因Tanner图中的短循环而性能受损。本文旨在通过硬件感知方法解决这些问题。

Method: 利用偏置噪声辅助量子比特（ancilla）的硬件特性，使辅助量子比特仅受相位翻转（phase-flip）错误影响，而非同时受比特翻转（bit-flip）错误影响。通过自行车双变量码（bicycle bivariate codes）和循环超图乘积码（cyclic hypergraph product code）进行验证，比较不同噪声偏置条件下的有效容错距离和短循环数量。

Result: 当辅助量子比特仅受相位翻转错误时，传统症候提取电路的有效容错距离显著提高，短循环数量显著降低。在电路噪声为2×10⁻³且辅助量子比特的比特翻转错误概率比相位翻转错误低50倍的条件下，逻辑错误率实现近一个数量级的改进。

Conclusion: 本文证明了在无法维持完全偏置的条件下，使用偏置噪声量子比特仍能获得显著且实用的量子纠错优势，为QLDPC码的实际实现提供了重要指导。

Abstract: Remarkable technical progress has made high-rate, high-distance, quantum low-density parity-check codes (QLDPC) promising candidates for scalable quantum computing. However, it is hard to design low-depth syndrome extraction circuits that do not spread errors from ancilla qubits to multiple data qubits, also known as hook errors, for general QLDPC codes. Additionally, widely used decoders for these codes based on belief propagation are impaired due to short loops in the Tanner graph. Here, we investigate a hardware-aware approach to avoid these hooks and loops using biased noise ancillas. Using examples of bicycle bivariate codes and a cyclic hypergraph product code, which have been widely considered for practical application, we show that the effective fault-distance of the conventional syndrome extraction circuit can be significantly higher and the number of short loops can be significantly lower when the ancillas are subject to phase-flip errors only, compared to when they are also subject to bit-flip errors. This can result in almost an order of magnitude improvement in the logical error rate at circuit noise of $2\times 10^{-3}$ and when bit-flip errors in the ancilla are 50 times less likely than phase-flip errors. Our work demonstrates a significant and practical quantum error correction advantage with biased noise qubits in which full-bias cannot be maintained.

</details>


### [110] [Provably Efficient Learning of Fermionic Correlations under Particle-Number Symmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30601)
*Yuki Koizumi,Kaito Wada,Toshinori P. Takama,Nobuyuki Yoshioka*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出基于随机轨道旋转的粒子数守恒费米子阴影层析框架，证明可以仅用 $O_k(\eta^k/\varepsilon^2)$ 个样本同时估计所有 $k$ 体费米子关联，样本复杂度与系统尺寸 $N$ 无关，并建立了信息论下界 $Ω_k(\eta^k/\varepsilon^2)$，数值实验显示在 $N=100,\eta=20,\varepsilon=10^{-2}$ 时查询量比现有方法降低约一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 粒子数对称性对量子态施加了强结构约束，但此前尚不明确这种对称性是否能在集体学习局部关联中带来可证明的优势。

Method: 发展了基于随机轨道旋转的粒子数守恒费米子阴影层析框架，利用粒子数对称性设计测量协议，并进行信息论下界证明。

Result: 1) 所有 $k$ 体费米子关联的样本复杂度为 $O_k(\eta^k/\varepsilon^2)$，与 $N$ 无关；2) 匹配的下界为 $Ω_k(\eta^k/\varepsilon^2)$，证明 $(\eta^k,\varepsilon)$ 依赖在常数意义下最优；3) 数值计算表明，在 $N=100,\eta=20,\varepsilon=10^{-2}$ 时单体验关联估计查询量比现有方法降低约一个数量级。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了粒子数对称性在费米子可观测量估计中可证明的高效优势。

Abstract: Predicting local fermionic correlations is a central task in quantum many-body physics, as these correlations encode many physically relevant local observables. The ubiquitous particle-number symmetry imposes strong structural constraints on quantum states, suggesting that local correlations should be learned with fewer samples than by symmetry-agnostic approaches. However, it has remained unclear whether such a provable advantage exists in collective learning of local correlations. Here, we develop a framework of number-conserving fermionic-shadow tomography based on random orbital rotations. We prove that, for every given order $k$, we can simultaneously estimate {\it all} $k$-body fermionic correlations of an $N$-mode $η$-particle state with a given variance $\varepsilon^2$ using only $O_k(η^k/\varepsilon^2)$ samples, which are independent of the system size $N$. We further establish a matching information-theoretic lower bound $Ω_k(η^k/\varepsilon^2)$ for any adaptive protocol based on single-copy measurements, showing that the $(η^k,\varepsilon)$-dependence is optimal up to constants depending only on $k$. Furthermore, our numerical calculation shows that the proposal reduces the query count by roughly an order of magnitude compared with state-of-the-art methods for one-body correlation estimation in a system of $N=100$, $η=20$ at $\varepsilon=10^{-2}$. This work establishes a provably efficient advantage of particle-number symmetry for fermionic observables estimation.

</details>


### [111] [Repetition-code-based readout error detection and correction across hardware platforms and generations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30606)
*Csaba Czabán,Orsolya Kálmán,Sergey N. Filippov,Zoltán Zimborás*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了重复码读出错误检测与纠错技术在IBM超导处理器和Quantinuum离子阱处理器上的跨平台、跨世代表现，发现该技术能提升所有设备的读出保真度，但额外冗余的价值高度依赖底层硬件的门错误率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 读出错误是当前量子处理器的主要噪声源之一，既影响期望值估计也影响基于采样的应用。现有读出错误缓解方法仅能校正期望值等聚合量，无法在单次测量层面进行操作。本文旨在通过重复码编码技术实现逐次测量的读出错误检测与纠正，并系统评估其在不同硬件平台上的实际效果。

Method: 在测量前将每个数据量子比特与辅助量子比特进行冗余编码（重复码），测量后通过后选择或多数投票对结果比特串进行解码。作者在IBM Heron r1-r3超导处理器和Quantinuum H1/H2离子阱处理器上，独立改变码距、硬件代次和编码布局，系统评估了读出错误检测与纠错的性能。

Result: 1. 在所有测试设备上，读出错误检测与纠错均能提升读出保真度，即使在未编码基线随硬件更新显著改善的情况下。2. 额外冗余的价值高度依赖底层硬件：超导处理器上编码引入的额外门错误迅速抵消其收益；离子阱处理器上极低的门错误率允许使用更大的码距并保持优势。

Conclusion: 重复码读出错误检测与纠错是一种通用有效的方法，但其最佳码距选择和实际收益与硬件门错误率密切相关。对于超导处理器，较小码距或检测模式更优；对于离子阱处理器，更大码距仍能带来增益。

Abstract: Readout errors are one of the dominant sources of noise in current quantum processors, limiting both expectation-value estimation and sampling-based applications. Since they affect only the classical measurement outcomes, they can be addressed using classical coding techniques: immediately before measurement, each data qubit is redundantly encoded with ancilla qubits, and the resulting bit string is decoded either by post-selection or by majority voting. Unlike conventional readout error mitigation, which corrects only aggregate quantities such as expectation values, this approach operates on individual measurement shots and can therefore produce approximately corrected samples. We present a systematic cross-platform and cross-generation experimental evaluation of repetition-code readout error detection and correction. We benchmark the same protocol on IBM Heron r1-r3 superconducting processors and Quantinuum H1 and H2 trapped-ion processors while independently varying the code distance, hardware generation, and encoding layout. We find that both error detection and correction improve readout fidelity on every device and generation tested, even as the unencoded baseline improves substantially across successive hardware releases. At the same time, the value of additional redundancy depends strongly on the underlying hardware. On superconducting processors, the extra gate errors introduced by the encoding rapidly offset its benefits, whereas on trapped-ion processors the much lower gate error rates allow larger code distances to remain advantageous.

</details>


### [112] [Provable random-matrix spectral ramp in a static, geometrically local Hamiltonian](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30635)
*Matteo Ippoliti*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过将双酉电路中的Floquet准能谱嵌入到静态局域哈密顿量的能谱中，首次证明了在有限局域希尔伯特空间维度的静态几何局域多体系统中存在谱斜坡（随机矩阵斜坡）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子混沌通常与量子系统能谱中随机矩阵统计的出现相关联，谱形状因子（SFF）的线性增长区（斜坡）是重要诊断工具。此前仅在双酉电路（Floquet系统）中证明过存在随机矩阵斜坡，但在静态几何局域哈密顿量中尚未有严格证明。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 基于Bertini、Kos和Prosen（BKP）的双酉电路结果，结合Feynman-Kitaev钟构造的一个变体，将双酉电路的Floquet准能谱嵌入到静态局域哈密顿量的能谱中，并证明后者在对称性扇区内的连通SFF继承了BKP斜坡。

Result: 首次在静态、几何局域、有限局域希尔伯特空间维度的多体哈密顿量中证明了谱斜坡的存在。

Conclusion: 这项工作将双酉电路中的量子混沌行为（随机矩阵斜坡）推广到了静态哈密顿量系统，为理解静态多体系统中的量子混沌提供了新的严格理论基础。

Abstract: Quantum chaos is commonly associated with the emergence of random-matrix statistics in the spectra of quantum systems. A useful diagnostic is provided by the spectral form factor (SFF), which for random matrix ensembles displays a universal linear-growth regime (`ramp'). In the last decade, a landmark result by Bertini, Kos and Prosen (BKP) identified for the first time a class of geometrically local quantum dynamics of finite-dimensional particles where the SFF provably exhibits a random-matrix ramp: periodically driven (Floquet) qudit chains whose evolution is described by `dual-unitary' circuits. Here, building on the BKP result and on a recently proposed variant of the Feynman-Kitaev clock construction, we obtain a spectral ramp in a class of static, geometrically local Hamiltonians. Our strategy is to embed the Floquet quasienergy spectrum of a dual-unitary circuit into the energy spectrum of a static local Hamiltonian, and to prove that the latter's connected SFF inherits the BKP ramp within a symmetry sector. This is to our knowledge the first proof of a spectral ramp in a time-independent, geometrically local many-body system with finite local Hilbert space dimension.

</details>


### [113] [Authentication in Quantum Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30636)
*Christopher Battarbee,Suchetana Goswami,Elham Kashefi,Mina Doosti*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文综述了量子通信中的认证任务，涵盖经典消息认证、量子消息认证和实体认证三种主要类型，比较了代表性协议的安全性假设、设置要求、可组合性和可扩展性，并通过案例分析阐述了认证在QKD及其他协议中的作用，强调认证资源并非量子网络的固有局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子通信中认证任务的不同类型（经典消息认证、量子消息认证、实体认证）在文献中常被混淆，缺乏系统性的比较和梳理，需要一份全面的综述来厘清各类认证的功能、假设和适用场景。

Method: 采用综述研究方法，系统梳理三类认证协议，从安全性假设、设置要求、可组合性、大规模/动态网络可扩展性等维度进行比较分析，并通过具体案例（如QKD中的认证）进行深入讨论。

Result: 认证资源并非量子网络的固有局限；每个协议依赖特定的认证资源，安全声明只有在明确认证资源及其部署假设时才有意义。现有经典和量子文献已提供多种可支持不同应用的量子安全认证方案。

Conclusion: 选择认证方案时需要仔细匹配所需功能、假设和安全保证；明确认证资源及其部署假设是安全声明有意义的前提。

Abstract: In this review, we survey the cryptographic task of authentication from the perspective of quantum communication. We review three main flavours of authentication that are often conflated in the literature: authentication of classical messages, authentication of quantum messages, and entity authentication, also covering recent hardware-assisted approaches. We compare representative protocols for each functionality in terms of their security assumptions, set-up requirements, composability, and scalability in large or dynamic networks, and use these criteria to identify and recommend suitable candidates. Finally, applications are surveyed: we provide a detailed case study of authentication and quantum key distribution (QKD), then extend the discussion to protocols beyond QKD, where the role of authentication is more complex. Our take-home message is that an authentication requirement is not an intrinsic limitation of quantum networks: as with all secure communication, each protocol relies on a particular authentication resource, and the security claim of that protocol is meaningful only once the authentication resource and its deployment assumptions are made explicit. At the same time, the existing classical and quantum literature already offers a range of quantum-secure authentication schemes, which can support different applications when carefully matched to the required functionality, assumptions, and security guarantees.

</details>
